  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mich adalah dewa laut berwujud ikan yang dipuja penduduk asli Kamchatka, dewa yang hanya memikirkan dirinya sendiri.",
    "en": "Mich is a fish-shaped sea god worshipped by the indigenous people of Kamchatka, a god who thinks only of himself.",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Penduduk asli Kamchatka memuja laut dengan nama Mich dan menggambarkan dewa itu berwujud ikan; mereka menganggapnya salah satu dewa pertama. Mich hanya memikirkan dirinya sendiri: bila ia mengirim ikan ke pantai, itu hanya agar orang mencarikan kayu untuk kanonya, bukan untuk menolong manusia.",
      "en": "The indigenous people of Kamchatka worship the sea as Mich and depict the god as a fish, counting him among the first deities. Mich thinks only of himself: if he sends fish to the shore, it is only so people will fetch wood for his canoes, not to help them.",

      ]

    {
      "id": "mich-c01",
      "source_id": "mich-s1",
      "quote": "Con el nombre de Mich rinden culto al mar los indígenas de Kamchatka, representando y adornando a tal divinidad en la forma de un pez. Lo consideran como una de las primeras deidades.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penduduk asli Kamchatka memuja laut dengan nama Mich, menggambarkan dewa itu berwujud ikan, dan menganggapnya salah satu dewa pertama.",
        "en": "The indigenous people of Kamchatka worship the sea as Mich, depicting the god as a fish and counting it among the first deities."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mich-c02",
      "source_id": "mich-s1",
      "quote": "Mich solo piensa en sí mismo, por lo que aquellos pueblos no esperan del dios ningún beneficio, pues si envía los peces a las orillas del mar, solo es para que le busquen y lleven la madera para hacer sus canoas y no para ser útiles a los hombres.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mich hanya memikirkan dirinya sendiri, sehingga orang tidak mengharap kebaikan darinya; bila ia mengirim ikan ke pantai, itu hanya agar orang mencarikan kayu untuk kanonya.",
        "en": "Mich thinks only of himself, so people expect no favours; if he sends fish to the shore, it is only so they will fetch him wood for his canoes."
      }
    }

  ],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Molsosa adalah binatang penjaga hutan bertubuh lumut dalam mitologi Katalan yang meninggalkan jejak lumut hijau dan memadamkan api.",
    "en": "The Molsosa is a moss-covered forest-guardian beast of Catalan mythology that leaves a trail of green moss and puts out fires.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "molsosa-c01",
      "molsosa-c02",
      "molsosa-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Para pembuat arang di Cerdanya menggambarkan Molsosa sebagai binatang berkaki empat bertubuh lumut atau bulu seperti rumput, yang meninggalkan jejak lumut hijau lembap di mana pun ia lewat. Ia dipandang sebagai pendewaan kelembapan hutan yang menumbuhkan lumut.",
      "en": "The charcoal-burners of Cerdanya described the Molsosa as a four-legged beast covered in moss or grassy fur, leaving a trail of damp green moss wherever it went. It is seen as a deification of the forest moisture that grows moss.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "molsosa-c01",
        "molsosa-c02",
        "molsosa-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sebagai penjaga hutan, ia takut pada api dan asap dan berusaha memadamkannya. Karena itu para pembuat arang takut kepadanya dan percaya ia menyerang mereka karena menebang pohon.",
      "en": "As guardian of the forests it fears fire and smoke and tries to put them out. So the charcoal-burners feared it and believed it attacked them for cutting trees.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "molsosa-c04",
        "molsosa-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,

      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "molsosa-c01",
      "source_id": "molsosa-s1",
      "quote": "La Molsosa és un personatge de la mitologia catalana que els carboners de la Cerdanya descrivien com una mena de bèstia de quatre potes, amb el cos cobert de molsa o d'un pelatge herbós.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Molsosa adalah tokoh mitologi Katalan yang oleh para pembuat arang di Cerdanya digambarkan sebagai binatang berkaki empat bertubuh lumut atau bulu seperti rumput.",
        "en": "The Molsosa is a figure of Catalan mythology that the charcoal-burners of Cerdanya described as a four-legged beast covered in moss or grassy fur."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "molsosa-c02",
      "source_id": "molsosa-s1",
      "quote": "La seva característica principal és que per allà on passava deixava un rastre o camí de molsa verda, fresca i humida.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ciri utamanya, di mana pun ia lewat, ia meninggalkan jejak lumut hijau yang segar dan lembap.",
        "en": "Its main feature is that wherever it passed it left a trail of fresh, damp green moss."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "molsosa-c03",
      "source_id": "molsosa-s1",
      "quote": "Es tracta d'una divinització de la humitat vegetal dels boscos que produeix la molsa",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Molsosa adalah pendewaan kelembapan tumbuhan hutan yang menumbuhkan lumut.",
        "en": "It is a deification of the forest's plant moisture that produces moss."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "molsosa-c04",
      "source_id": "molsosa-s1",
      "quote": "Com que és la guardiana dels boscos, l'espanten el foc i les fumeres, que procura apagar i, per això, era temuda pels vells carboners cerdans",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai penjaga hutan, ia takut pada api dan asap yang berusaha dipadamkannya, sehingga ditakuti para pembuat arang tua di Cerdanya.",
        "en": "As guardian of the forests it fears fire and smoke, which it tries to put out, and so was feared by the old charcoal-burners of Cerdanya."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "molsosa-c05",
      "source_id": "molsosa-s1",
      "quote": "els carboners creien que els atacava perquè tallaven els arbres",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para pembuat arang percaya ia menyerang mereka karena mereka menebang pohon.",
        "en": "The charcoal-burners believed it attacked them because they cut down trees."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Monachicchio adalah peri usil bertopi merah dalam cerita rakyat Lucania, yang di Grassano dianggap roh anak yang mati sebelum dibaptis.",
    "en": "The Monachicchio is a red-capped mischievous sprite of Lucanian folklore, held in Grassano to be the spirit of a child who died before baptism.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "monachicchio-c01",
      "monachicchio-c05",
      "monachicchio-c08"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Monachicchio adalah tokoh cerita rakyat Lucania yang gambarannya berbeda di tiap daerah Basilicata. Menurut tradisi Grassano, ia roh anak yang mati sebelum dibaptis, berwajah elok, berhati lembut, dan memakai topi kecil merah bernama u cuppulicchi; siapa yang merebut topi itu akan mendapat koin emas di dalamnya. Menurut tradisi Matera, ia peri usil yang mengikat surai kuda dan ekor keledai, tetapi tidak pernah menimbulkan kerugian serius, dan keisengannya hanya berhenti bila topinya direbut.",
      "en": "The Monachicchio is a figure of Lucanian folklore whose description varies across Basilicata. In the Grassano tradition he is the spirit of a child who died before baptism, handsome and gentle, wearing a little red cap called u cuppulicchi; whoever snatches it gets the gold coins inside. In the Matera tradition he is a mischievous sprite that ties horses' manes and donkeys' tails but never does serious harm, and his pranks stop only when his cap is taken.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "monachicchio-c01",
        "monachicchio-c02",
        "monachicchio-c05",
        "monachicchio-c06",
        "monachicchio-c07",
        "monachicchio-c08",
        "monachicchio-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Monachicchio banyak kesamaan dengan Scazzamurrieddhru dan Munaciello dari Napoli, dan asal mitosnya dicari pada agama Romawi, yang memberi Lares dan Penates tugas melindungi orang mati dan rumah.",
      "en": "The Monachicchio has much in common with the Scazzamurrieddhru and the Neapolitan Munaciello, and its origin is traced to Roman religion, which entrusted the Lares and Penates with protecting the dead and the home.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c01",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "Il Monachicchio è il nome, in forma italianizzata, di un personaggio del folklore lucano.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monachicchio adalah nama yang diitalianisasi untuk tokoh cerita rakyat Lucania.",
        "en": "Monachicchio is the Italianised name of a figure of Lucanian folklore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c02",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "il Monachicchio non solo ha una sua precisa identità, ma le sue descrizioni variano da zona a zona della Basilicata.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monachicchio punya identitas sendiri, dan gambarannya berbeda dari satu daerah Basilicata ke daerah lain.",
        "en": "The Monachicchio has its own identity, and descriptions of it vary across Basilicata."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c03",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "Sebbene l'origine e l'aspetto del suo mito hanno molto in comune con quelli dello Scazzamurrieddhru e del Munaciello napoletano",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal dan wujudnya banyak kesamaan dengan Scazzamurrieddhru dan Munaciello dari Napoli.",
        "en": "Its origin and look have much in common with the Scazzamurrieddhru and the Neapolitan Munaciello."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c04",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "L'origine del mito va cercata nelle tradizioni religiose romane, che attribuivano ai Lari e ai Penati la protezione dei defunti e della casa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal mitosnya dicari pada agama Romawi yang memberi Lares dan Penates tugas melindungi orang mati dan rumah.",
        "en": "The myth's origin lies in Roman religion, which entrusted the Lares and Penates with protecting the dead and the home."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c05",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "Seconda la tradizione di Grassano, in provincia di Matera, il Monachicchio è lo spirito di un bambino morto prima di ricevere il battesimo. Di bell'aspetto e di carattere gentile, porta in testa un berrettino di color rosso, detto u cuppulicchi",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tradisi Grassano di provinsi Matera, Monachicchio adalah roh anak yang mati sebelum dibaptis, berwajah elok dan berhati lembut, yang memakai topi kecil merah bernama u cuppulicchi.",
        "en": "In the tradition of Grassano, province of Matera, the Monachicchio is the spirit of a child who died before baptism, handsome and gentle, wearing a little red cap called u cuppulicchi."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c06",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "sa che se i compagni di gioco gli togliessero u cuppulicchi, ne raccoglierebbero le monetine d'oro che questo contiene.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bila teman bermainnya merebut topinya, mereka akan mendapat koin emas kecil di dalamnya.",
        "en": "If playmates snatched his cap, they would collect the little gold coins inside it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c07",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "legare i peli della coda di asini e muli o la criniera dei cavalli",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia suka mengikat bulu ekor keledai dan bagal atau surai kuda.",
        "en": "He likes to tie the tail hairs of donkeys and mules or horses' manes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c08",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "Secondo la tradizione del materano, il Monachicchio è un folletto che fa dispetti, i quali, però, mai causano problemi o danni seri.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tradisi daerah Matera, Monachicchio peri usil yang keisengannya tidak pernah menimbulkan masalah serius.",
        "en": "In the Matera tradition the Monachicchio is a mischievous sprite whose pranks never cause serious harm."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monachicchio-c09",
      "source_id": "monachicchio-s1",
      "quote": "L'unico modo per porre fine ai loro scherzi è privarlo del cappello.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Satu-satunya cara menghentikan keisengannya adalah merebut topinya.",
        "en": "The only way to end its pranks is to take away its cap."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mulher marinha adalah putri duyung berkaki kambing dalam legenda Portugis yang dalam tradisi Azores menikahi lelaki darat dan membawa keberuntungan.",
    "en": "The mulher marinha is a goat-footed mermaid of Portuguese legend who in Azorean tradition marries a man of the land and brings good fortune.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "mulher-marinha-c01",
      "mulher-marinha-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mulher marinha adalah makhluk mitos dalam legenda dan tradisi rakyat Portugis, semacam putri duyung berkaki kambing: dari pinggang ke bawah ia bersisik, dengan kaki kambing dan sirip di tungkainya. Ia dikaitkan dengan mouras encantadas, Nereid, dan Oceanid.",
      "en": "The mulher marinha is a mythical being of Portuguese legend and folk tradition, a kind of goat-footed mermaid: scaly from the waist down, with goat's feet and fins on her legs. She is associated with the enchanted mouras, Nereids and Oceanids.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mulher-marinha-c01",
        "mulher-marinha-c02",
        "mulher-marinha-c04"
      ]
    },
    {

      "en": "In Azorean tradition she leaves the water, becomes a woman and marries a farmer or fisherman, bringing joy, fertility and fortune.",

      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "mulher-marinha-c01",
      "source_id": "mulher-marinha-s1",
      "quote": "A mulher marinha (ou apenas, marinha) é um ser mítico das lendas portuguesas e da tradição popular. Pode ser descrita como uma espécie de sereia com pés de cabra",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mulher marinha adalah makhluk mitos dalam legenda dan tradisi rakyat Portugis, semacam putri duyung berkaki kambing.",
        "en": "The mulher marinha is a mythical being of Portuguese legend and folk tradition, a kind of mermaid with goat's feet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mulher-marinha-c02",
      "source_id": "mulher-marinha-s1",
      "quote": "Da cintura para baixo toda estava coberta de escamas, e os pés eram do feitio de cabra, com barbatanas pelas pernas",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dari pinggang ke bawah ia bersisik, kakinya berbentuk kaki kambing, dengan sirip di sepanjang tungkainya.",
        "en": "From the waist down she was covered in scales, with goat-shaped feet and fins along her legs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mulher-marinha-c03",
      "source_id": "mulher-marinha-s1",
      "quote": "surge em tradições do arquipélagos dos Açores. Aqui, é uma entidade híbrida que sai da água e se transforma em mulher e casando com um homem da terra, agricultor ou pescador, traz-lhe grande alegria, fertilidade e fortuna.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam tradisi Kepulauan Azores, ia makhluk campuran yang keluar dari air, menjelma perempuan, dan menikahi petani atau nelayan, membawakan kebahagiaan, kesuburan, dan keberuntungan.",
        "en": "In Azorean tradition she is a hybrid being who leaves the water, becomes a woman and marries a farmer or fisherman, bringing joy, fertility and fortune."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mulher-marinha-c04",
      "source_id": "mulher-marinha-s1",
      "quote": "onde também é associada às mouras encantadas, Nereidas e Oceânides",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga dikaitkan dengan mouras encantadas, Nereid, dan Oceanid.",
        "en": "She is also associated with the enchanted mouras, Nereids and Oceanids."
      }
    }

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Na Ruixa adalah peri air dari Kepulauan Balears yang konon tinggal di gua Mallorca dan menangkap pelaut dengan mantel birunya saat badai.",
    "en": "Na Ruixa is a water fairy of the Balearic Islands said to live in caves on Mallorca and catch sailors in storms with her blue cloak.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "na-ruixa-c01",
      "na-ruixa-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Na Ruixa atau Ruixa Mantells adalah peri dari mitologi Balears yang mungkin berakar pada tradisi lisan setempat. Ia konon tinggal di gua-gua Mallorca, mungkin di teluk Pollença, dan memakai mantel biru lebar untuk menangkap pelaut lengah di tengah badai.",
      "en": "Na Ruixa or Ruixa Mantells is a fairy of Balearic mythology possibly rooted in local oral tradition. She is said to live in caves on Mallorca, perhaps in the bay of Pollença, and wears a wide blue cloak to capture unwary sailors in storms.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Nonell de la neu adalah raksasa salju dalam mitologi Katalan, pemuda yang menjadi anjing putih raksasa, membawa salju, dan melindungi kawanan ternak.",
    "en": "Nonell de la neu is a snow giant of Catalan mythology, a youth turned into a giant white dog who brings the snow and protects the flocks.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "nonell-de-la-neu-c01",
      "nonell-de-la-neu-c02",
      "nonell-de-la-neu-c05"

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, Nonell de la neu adalah semacam raksasa salju. Dulunya ia pemuda tampan berjanggut lebat dan berambut pirang panjang, yang kemudian berubah menjadi anjing putih besar seukuran kuda. Ia muncul tak lama setelah salju pertama, karena dialah yang membawa salju dari satu tempat ke tempat lain.",
      "en": "In Catalan mythology Nonell de la neu is a kind of snow giant. Once a handsome youth with a thick beard and long blond hair, he became a great white dog the size of a horse. He appears soon after the first snowfall, since he carries the snow from place to place.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nonell-de-la-neu-c01",
        "nonell-de-la-neu-c02",
        "nonell-de-la-neu-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia dianggap damai: ternak tidur tenang saat ia mendekat karena tahu Nonell melindungi mereka. Tokoh ini berkerabat dengan Basajaun dari Basque dan mirip Yeti; Joan Amades mencatat satu versi legendanya di La Pobla de Lillet.",
      "en": "He is considered peaceful: flocks sleep easily when he comes near, knowing Nonell protects them. The figure is related to the Basque Basajaun and resembles the Yeti; Joan Amades recorded a version of the legend at La Pobla de Lillet.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "nonell-de-la-neu-c03",
        "nonell-de-la-neu-c04",

      "source_id": "nonell-de-la-neu-s1",
      "quote": "En la mitologia catalana, en Nonell de la neu és una mena de gegant de les neus, un noi que es va convertir en un gran gos blanc, de la mida d'un cavall.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, Nonell de la neu adalah semacam raksasa salju, seorang pemuda yang berubah menjadi anjing putih besar seukuran kuda.",
        "en": "In Catalan mythology Nonell de la neu is a kind of snow giant, a young man who turned into a great white dog the size of a horse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nonell-de-la-neu-c02",
      "source_id": "nonell-de-la-neu-s1",
      "quote": "Sempre apareix poc després de la primera nevada, ja que, de fet, és ell mateix qui porta la neu d'un lloc a l'altre",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia selalu muncul tak lama setelah salju pertama, karena dialah yang membawa salju dari satu tempat ke tempat lain.",
        "en": "He always appears soon after the first snowfall, since it is he who carries the snow from place to place."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nonell-de-la-neu-c03",
      "source_id": "nonell-de-la-neu-s1",
      "quote": "Joan Amades va recollir una versió de la llegenda a la Pobla de Lillet.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Joan Amades mencatat satu versi legendanya di La Pobla de Lillet.",
        "en": "Joan Amades recorded a version of the legend at La Pobla de Lillet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nonell-de-la-neu-c04",
      "source_id": "nonell-de-la-neu-s1",
      "quote": "El personatge està emparentat amb el Basajaun basc i és similar al Ieti o abominable home de les neus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh ini berkerabat dengan Basajaun dari Basque dan mirip Yeti.",
        "en": "The figure is related to the Basque Basajaun and similar to the Yeti."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nonell-de-la-neu-c05",
      "source_id": "nonell-de-la-neu-s1",
      "quote": "Se'l considera pacífic i es diu que quan s'acosta als ramats, les seves esquelles sonen i poden dormir tranquil·les perquè saben que en Nonell les protegeix.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dianggap damai; bila mendekati kawanan ternak, lonceng ternak berbunyi dan ternak dapat tidur tenang karena tahu Nonell melindungi mereka.",
        "en": "He is considered peaceful; when he nears the flocks their bells ring and they sleep easily, knowing Nonell protects them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "nonell-de-la-neu-c06",
      "source_id": "nonell-de-la-neu-s1",
      "quote": "En Nonell era un xicot de gran bellesa, amb barba poblada i llargs cabells rossos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dulunya Nonell pemuda yang sangat tampan, berjanggut lebat dan berambut pirang panjang.",
        "en": "Nonell was once a very handsome young man with a thick beard and long blond hair."
      }
    }

    "id": "Ogre de la Caputxa Negra adalah momok berwajah tertutup dalam cerita rakyat Valencia yang menculik anak-anak dan dapat dibunuh dengan sinar matahari.",
    "en": "The Ogre de la Caputxa Negra is a face-covered bogey of Valencian folklore who kidnaps children and can be killed by sunlight.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Ogre de la Caputxa Negra, \"Ogre Berkerudung Hitam\", adalah momok penakut anak dalam imajinasi rakyat Valencia: makhluk menakutkan berwajah tertutup yang membawa pergi semua anak yang ditemuinya. Ia tinggal di istana tempat ia menyembunyikan harta dan seorang putri tersihir yang menjadi putri duyung bila keluar dari istana, dan menurut Francesc Gisbert ia selalu mengalungkan kunci istana di lehernya. Ia dapat dibunuh dengan membuat sinar matahari mengenai wajahnya.",
      "en": "The Ogre de la Caputxa Negra is a child-scaring bogey of Valencian folk imagination: a terrifying face-covered creature who carries off every child he meets. He lives in a palace hiding a treasure and an enchanted princess who turns into a mermaid if she leaves, and according to Francesc Gisbert he always wears the palace key around his neck. He can be killed by making sunlight strike his face.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Oilar, \"ayam jantan\" dalam bahasa Basque, muncul dalam legenda Basque sebagai pengusir makhluk gaib dan kadang wujud roh jahat.",
    "en": "Oilar, Basque for \"rooster\", appears in Basque legends as a herald that sends spirits fleeing and sometimes as the form of an evil spirit.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "oilar-c01",
      "oilar-c02",
      "oilar-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Oilar berarti \"ayam jantan\" dalam bahasa Basque, dan hewan ini sering muncul dalam legenda Basque. Kokoknya menandai larinya makhluk gaib seperti lamiñak, tetapi kadang roh jahat, Gaiskiñe, justru mengambil wujud ayam jantan.",
      "en": "Oilar means \"rooster\" in Basque, and the bird often appears in Basque legends. Its crow sends spirits such as the lamiñak fleeing, yet sometimes the evil spirit, Gaiskiñe, takes the rooster's form.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "oilar-c01",
        "oilar-c02",
        "oilar-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Bila seseorang menderita penyakit misterius, bulu bantalnya diperiksa: gumpalan berbentuk ayam jantan utuh berarti penyakit tak tersembuhkan, sedangkan bila kepalanya belum terbentuk, bulu itu dibakar di persimpangan jalan agar penyakit sembuh.",
      "en": "When someone suffers a mysterious illness, the pillow feathers are examined: a clump shaped like a whole rooster means the illness is incurable, while if the head has not formed, the feathers are burned at a crossroads to cure it.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "oilar-c04",
        "oilar-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "source_id": "oilar-s1",
      "quote": "Oilar (on peut trouver aussi le nom d'Ollaar) signifie « coq » en basque. Il n'est pas rare de rencontrer le coq dans les légendes basques.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Oilar (juga Ollaar) berarti \"ayam jantan\" dalam bahasa Basque, hewan yang cukup sering muncul dalam legenda Basque.",
        "en": "Oilar (also Ollaar) means \"rooster\" in Basque, an animal often found in Basque legends."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oilar-c02",
      "source_id": "oilar-s1",
      "quote": "Son chant annonce la fuite des génies comme les lamiñak",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kokoknya menandai larinya makhluk gaib seperti lamiñak.",
        "en": "Its crow announces the flight of spirits such as the lamiñak."
      }

    {
      "id": "oilar-c03",
      "source_id": "oilar-s1",
      "quote": "Parfois l'esprit malin (\"Gaiskiñe\" en basque) revêt l'aspect de cet animal.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang roh jahat (Gaiskiñe dalam bahasa Basque) mengambil wujud ayam jantan.",
        "en": "Sometimes the evil spirit (Gaiskiñe in Basque) takes the form of this animal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oilar-c04",
      "source_id": "oilar-s1",
      "quote": "Si quelqu'un souffre d'une maladie mystérieuse il faut examiner les plumes de son oreiller ou du traversin. Si on trouve un amas qui épouse la forme d'un coq entier, on considère que la maladie est incurable.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bila seseorang menderita penyakit misterius, bulu di bantalnya diperiksa; bila ditemukan gumpalan berbentuk ayam jantan utuh, penyakit itu dianggap tak tersembuhkan.",
        "en": "If someone suffers a mysterious illness, the feathers of their pillow are examined; a clump shaped like a whole rooster means the illness is incurable."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oilar-c05",
      "source_id": "oilar-s1",
      "quote": "Si la tête n'est pas formée, il faut porter les plumes à une croisée de chemins et les y brûler : alors la maladie sera guéri.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bila kepalanya belum terbentuk, bulu itu dibakar di persimpangan jalan dan penyakit itu pun sembuh.",
        "en": "If the head is not yet formed, the feathers are burned at a crossroads and the illness is cured."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Olhapim adalah makhluk kecil bermata empat dalam cerita rakyat Portugis yang dapat melihat ke segala arah.",
    "en": "The olhapim is a tiny four-eyed being of Portuguese folklore that can see in every direction.",
    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Olhapim adalah makhluk kecil dalam cerita rakyat Portugis, semacam roh, peri, atau hantu, yang juga disebut anaio. Mereka bermata empat, dua di depan dan dua di belakang, sehingga dapat melihat ke segala arah. Mereka berbeda dari olharapo, yang berukuran raksasa.",
      "en": "The olhapim is a tiny being of Portuguese folklore, a kind of spirit, goblin or ghost, also called anaio. Olhapins have four eyes, two in front and two behind, so they see in every direction. They differ from the olharapos, which are giants.",

      ]

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "olhapim-c01",
      "source_id": "olhapim-s1",
      "quote": "Os olhapins são seres do folclore popular português. São muito baixinhos, por isso também são chamados de anaios. São uma espécie de espíritos, duendes ou assombrações.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Olhapim adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Portugis yang sangat kecil, sehingga juga disebut anaio; mereka semacam roh, peri, atau hantu.",
        "en": "Olhapins are beings of Portuguese folklore, very small and therefore also called anaios; they are a kind of spirit, goblin or ghost."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "olhapim-c02",
      "source_id": "olhapim-s1",
      "quote": "Os olhapins têm quatro olhos, dois para a frente e dois para trás por isso olham para todo o lado. Diferem dos olharapos por serem muito pequenos e os olharapos serem gigantes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Olhapim bermata empat, dua di depan dan dua di belakang, sehingga dapat melihat ke segala arah; mereka berbeda dari olharapo karena olhapim sangat kecil, sedangkan olharapo raksasa.",
        "en": "Olhapins have four eyes, two in front and two behind, so they see in every direction; they differ from olharapos in being tiny, whereas olharapos are giants."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Olharapo adalah raksasa bermata satu pemakan manusia dalam dongeng Portugis, terutama dari Trás-os-Montes.",
    "en": "The olharapo is a one-eyed man-eating giant of Portuguese fairy tales, especially from Trás-os-Montes.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "olharapo-c01",
      "olharapo-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam dongeng Portugis, olharapo termasuk ras raksasa bermata satu besar di dahi, seperti kiklop. Menurut Alexandre Parafita, ia terutama muncul dalam dongeng dan legenda Trás-os-Montes sebagai raksasa pemakan manusia yang kejam dan buas.",
      "en": "In Portuguese fairy tales the olharapo belongs to a race of giants with one great eye on the forehead, like a cyclops. According to Alexandre Parafita, it appears especially in the tales and legends of Trás-os-Montes as a violent, fierce man-eating giant.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "olharapo-c01",
        "olharapo-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Tradisi rakyat juga mengenal olharapa dan olhapim, dan di Vinhais ada peribahasa: \"Di negeri para olhapim, yang bermata dua menjadi raja.\"",
      "en": "Folk tradition also knows the olharapa and the olhapim, and Vinhais has a proverb: \"In the land of the olhapins, he who has two eyes is king.\"",
      "claim_ids": [
        "olharapo-c03",
        "olharapo-c04"
      ]
    }

    {
      "id": "olharapo-c01",
      "source_id": "olharapo-s1",
      "quote": "Nos contos de fadas portugueses o olharapo pertence a uma raça de gigantes que tem um grande olho na testa (Ciclope), e é parecido com o alicórnio.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam dongeng Portugis, olharapo termasuk ras raksasa bermata satu besar di dahi, seperti kiklop.",
        "en": "In Portuguese fairy tales the olharapo belongs to a race of giants with one great eye on the forehead, like a cyclops."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "olharapo-c02",
      "source_id": "olharapo-s1",
      "quote": "Segundo Alexandre Parafita, o olharapo é referenciado, especialmente, em contos e lendas transmontanos que o apresentam como um gigante antropófago, violento e feroz, que tem a particularidade de possuir um único olho no centro da testa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Alexandre Parafita, olharapo terutama muncul dalam dongeng dan legenda Trás-os-Montes sebagai raksasa pemakan manusia yang kejam dan buas, bermata satu di tengah dahi.",
        "en": "According to Alexandre Parafita, the olharapo appears especially in tales and legends of Trás-os-Montes as a violent, fierce man-eating giant with a single eye in the middle of the forehead."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "olharapo-c03",
      "source_id": "olharapo-s1",
      "quote": "A tradição popular reconhece ainda a olharapa e o olhapim.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tradisi rakyat juga mengenal olharapa (perempuan) dan olhapim.",
        "en": "Folk tradition also knows the olharapa and the olhapim."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "olharapo-c04",
      "source_id": "olharapo-s1",
      "quote": "para justificar um provérbio recorrente no concelho de Vinhais: “Na terra dos olhapins, quem tem dois olhos é rei”.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Vinhais ada peribahasa: \"Di negeri para olhapim, yang bermata dua menjadi raja.\"",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Orcolat adalah raksasa gua dalam tradisi Carnia dan Friuli yang dipercaya menyebabkan gempa bumi; namanya menjadi sebutan untuk gempa Friuli 1976.",
    "en": "The Orcolat is a cave-dwelling giant of the tradition of Carnia and Friuli believed to cause earthquakes; its name became a byword for the 1976 Friuli earthquake.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "orcolat-c01",
      "orcolat-c02",
      "orcolat-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Orcolât, \"orc jelek\", adalah makhluk legendaris dalam tradisi Carnia dan Friuli-Venezia Giulia: makhluk mirip orc dan raksasa yang tinggal di gua-gua Carnia. Ia dikaitkan dengan kekuatan dan kekerasan alam, dituduh menyebabkan gempa bumi, dan dianggap penjaga rahasia pegunungan Alpen Carnia.",
      "en": "The Orcolât, \"nasty ogre\", is a legendary creature of the tradition of Carnia and Friuli-Venezia Giulia: an ogre- or giant-like being living in Carnia's caves. It was linked with nature's power and violence, blamed for earthquakes and considered the keeper of the Carnic Alps' secrets.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "orcolat-c01",
        "orcolat-c03",
        "orcolat-c04",
        "orcolat-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Setelah 1976, namanya menjadi sebutan untuk gempa yang melanda Friuli tahun itu. Pada 2026 sutradara Federico Savonitto membuat film dokumenter berjudul Orcolat.",
      "en": "After 1976 its name became a byword for that year's Friuli earthquake. In 2026 director Federico Savonitto made a documentary titled Orcolat.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "orcolat-c02",
        "orcolat-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "quote": "L'Orcolât (\"orcaccio\", spregiativo del friulano orcul, \"orco\") è una creatura leggendaria che appartiene alla tradizione della Carnia e del Friuli-Venezia Giulia.",
      "locator": null,

      "statement": {
        "id": "Orcolât (\"orc jelek\", bentuk merendahkan dari kata Friuli orcul, \"orc\") adalah makhluk legendaris dalam tradisi Carnia dan Friuli-Venezia Giulia.",
        "en": "The Orcolât (\"nasty ogre\", a pejorative of Friulian orcul, \"ogre\") is a legendary creature of the tradition of Carnia and Friuli-Venezia Giulia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "orcolat-c02",
      "source_id": "orcolat-s1",
      "quote": "Dopo il 1976 è divenuto sinonimo del terremoto che colpì il Friuli in quell'anno.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah 1976, namanya menjadi sebutan untuk gempa yang melanda Friuli pada tahun itu.",
        "en": "After 1976 it became a byword for the earthquake that struck Friuli that year."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "orcolat-c03",
      "source_id": "orcolat-s1",
      "quote": "L'Orcolat era una creatura assimilabile all'orco e al gigante abitante delle aree geografiche della Carnia, ove trovava dimora all'interno delle grotte.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orcolat makhluk mirip orc dan raksasa yang tinggal di gua-gua daerah Carnia.",
        "en": "The Orcolat was an ogre- or giant-like creature living in the caves of Carnia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "orcolat-c04",
      "source_id": "orcolat-s1",
      "quote": "La figura era associata alla forza della natura e alla sua violenza, venendogli affibiate la cause dei terremoti.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sosok ini dikaitkan dengan kekuatan dan kekerasan alam, dan dituduh menjadi penyebab gempa bumi.",

    {

      "source_id": "orcolat-s1",
      "quote": "L'essere mitologico viene inoltre considerato il custode dei segreti delle montagne delle Alpi Carniche.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga dianggap penjaga rahasia pegunungan Alpen Carnia.",
        "en": "It is also considered the keeper of the secrets of the Carnic Alps."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "orcolat-c06",
      "source_id": "orcolat-s1",
      "quote": "Nel 2026 il regista friulano Federico Savonitto ha realizzato il film documentario omonimo alla creatura mitologica, Orcolat, prodotto da Kublai Film con Rai Cinema e narrato da Bruno Pizzul.",
      "locator": null,

        "id": "Pada 2026 sutradara Friuli Federico Savonitto membuat film dokumenter berjudul Orcolat, diproduksi Kublai Film dan Rai Cinema dengan narasi Bruno Pizzul.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Oriande adalah peri dalam epos abad pertengahan, ibu baptis penyihir Maugis yang membesarkannya dan menyuruhnya membebaskan kuda Bayard.",
    "en": "Oriande is a fairy of medieval epic, godmother of the enchanter Maugis, who raises him and sets him to free the horse Bayard.",
    "claim_ids": [

      "oriande-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Oriande adalah peri dalam tradisi epos abad pertengahan, terutama chanson de geste Maugis d'Aigremont dari akhir abad ke-13. Ia menemukan Maugis kecil, putra Beuves d'Aigremont yang diculik Sorgalant, dan membesarkannya di Rosefleur dekat Etna di Sisilia.",
      "en": "Oriande is a fairy of medieval epic tradition, above all the late-13th-century chanson de geste Maugis d'Aigremont. She finds the child Maugis, son of Beuves d'Aigremont, kidnapped by Sorgalant, and raises him at Rosefleur near Etna in Sicily.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "oriande-c01",
        "oriande-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sebagai ibu baptis penyihir Maugis, ia memberinya ujian penentu: membebaskan kuda Bayard yang tertahan di bawah tanah di pulau gunung api Bocan.",
      "en": "As godmother of the enchanter Maugis, she sets him the decisive test of freeing the horse Bayard, held underground on the volcanic island of Bocan.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "oriande-c02",
        "oriande-c04"
      ]
    }

    {
      "id": "oriande-c01",
      "source_id": "oriande-s1",
      "quote": "Oriande est une fée de la tradition épique médiévale évoquée dans la chanson de geste des Quatre fils Aymon, et principalement la chanson de geste de Maugis d'Aigremont (fin XIIIe siècle) où elle joue un rôle important.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Oriande adalah peri dalam tradisi epos abad pertengahan, disebut dalam chanson de geste Quatre fils Aymon dan terutama Maugis d'Aigremont (akhir abad ke-13).",
        "en": "Oriande is a fairy of medieval epic tradition, mentioned in the chanson de geste of the Quatre fils Aymon and above all in Maugis d'Aigremont (late 13th century)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oriande-c02",
      "source_id": "oriande-s1",
      "quote": "Elle est la marraine de l'enchanteur Maugis. C'est elle qui l'élève et lui impose de conquérir le cheval Bayard pour obtenir son statut.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia ibu baptis penyihir Maugis, membesarkannya, dan mewajibkannya menaklukkan kuda Bayard untuk meraih statusnya.",
        "en": "She is godmother of the enchanter Maugis, raises him and requires him to win the horse Bayard to gain his status."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oriande-c03",
      "source_id": "oriande-s1",
      "quote": "Au début du récit, l’enfant Maugis, fils de Beuves d’Aigremont, enlevé par Sorgalant, est retrouvé par la fée Oriande, qui l’élève à Rosefleur (ou Rocheflor), près de Mongibel (Etna, Sicile).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di awal kisah, Maugis kecil, putra Beuves d'Aigremont yang diculik Sorgalant, ditemukan Oriande dan dibesarkan di Rosefleur dekat Mongibel (Etna, Sisilia).",
        "en": "At the start of the tale the child Maugis, son of Beuves d'Aigremont, kidnapped by Sorgalant, is found by Oriande, who raises him at Rosefleur near Mongibel (Etna, Sicily)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "oriande-c04",
      "source_id": "oriande-s1",
      "quote": "L’épreuve décisive qu’elle lui impose pour confirmer ses pouvoirs est de délivrer Bayard, retenu sous terre sur l’île volcanique de Bocan/Boucam",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ujian penentu yang diberikannya adalah membebaskan Bayard yang tertahan di bawah tanah di pulau gunung api Bocan.",
        "en": "The decisive test she sets is to free Bayard, held underground on the volcanic island of Bocan."
      }
    }

      "en": "Ornias is a demon of Jewish mythology. In the Testament of Solomon he tormented a youth by stealing half his wages and sucking his thumb until he wasted away, until Solomon received from God a sealed ring to bind him.",

      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Wasiat Salomo, Ornias menyiksa seorang pemuda dengan mencuri separuh upahnya dan mengisap ibu jari tangan kanannya hingga pemuda itu kurus.",
        "en": "In the Testament of Solomon, Ornias tormented a youth by stealing half his wages and sucking his right thumb until he grew thin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ornias-c03",
      "source_id": "ornias-s1",
      "quote": "Per tal motivo, Salomone pregò Iddio che gli concesse un anello con sigillo per poterlo sigillare.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena itu Salomo berdoa dan Tuhan memberinya cincin bermeterai untuk menyegel iblis itu.",
        "en": "So Solomon prayed, and God granted him a sealed ring to bind the demon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ornias-c04",
      "source_id": "ornias-s1",
      "quote": "svelò di essere colui che tenta gli uomini che si innamorano di una donna , trasformandosi in piacevoli donzelle e giocando con loro",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ornias mengaku menggoda lelaki yang jatuh cinta dengan menjelma gadis cantik dan mempermainkan mereka.",
        "en": "Ornias revealed that he tempts men in love by turning into pleasing maidens and toying with them."
      }
    }
  ],

Ia berleher merak, berparuh elang, berkaki angsa, dan bertubuh bangau, dengan sayap merah. Satu-satunya sumbernya adalah bestiarium Pierre de Beauvais.",
      "en": "The Orphan Bird is a legendary bird of medieval bestiaries said to live in a fictional sea, la mer darenoise, in India.     "id": "Manusia Lembu Wicklow adalah makhluk setengah manusia setengah lembu dari Irlandia abad ke-12 yang dicatat Gerald dari Wales dalam Topographia Hibernica.",
    "en": "The Ox Man of Wicklow is a 12th-century half-man, half-ox creature of Ireland recorded by Gerald of Wales in the Topographia Hibernica.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "ox-man-of-wicklow-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Manusia Lembu Wicklow adalah makhluk setengah manusia setengah lembu yang menurut sejarawan Giraud de Barri hidup di Wicklow, Irlandia, pada akhir abad ke-12. Giraud menggambarkannya berkepala besar botak seperti lembu, berwajah datar dengan dua mata besar, tanpa hidung melainkan dua lubang yang mengeluarkan bunyi seperti lenguhan banteng, dan bertangan serta berkaki kuku belah.",
      "en": "The Ox Man of Wicklow is a half-man, half-ox creature that, according to the historian Giraud de Barri, lived in Wicklow, Ireland, in the late 12th century. Giraud described a big bald ox-like head, a flat face with two huge eyes, two holes instead of a nose that made bull-like bellows, and cloven hooves for hands and feet.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ox-man-of-wicklow-c01",
        "ox-man-of-wicklow-c02",
        "ox-man-of-wicklow-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Makhluk ini sering datang ke istana baron Maurice FitzGerald di Black Castle, Wicklow, sampai akhirnya diculik dan dibunuh orang Irlandia.",
      "en": "It regularly visited the court of the baron Maurice FitzGerald at Black Castle, Wicklow, until it was seized and killed by the Irish.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "ox-man-of-wicklow-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ox-man-of-wicklow-c01",
      "source_id": "ox-man-of-wicklow-s1",
      "quote": "L'Homme-Bœuf de Wicklow (fl. fin du XIIe siècle) est une créature mi-homme mi-bœuf vivant à Wicklow, en Irlande, et cité par l'historien cambro-normand du XIIe siècle Giraud de Barri dans son ouvrage Topographia Hibernica",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manusia Lembu Wicklow (akhir abad ke-12) adalah makhluk setengah manusia setengah lembu di Wicklow, Irlandia, yang disebut sejarawan Kambro-Norman Giraud de Barri (Gerald dari Wales) dalam Topographia Hibernica.",
        "en": "The Ox Man of Wicklow (late 12th century) is a half-man, half-ox creature of Wicklow, Ireland, mentioned by the Cambro-Norman historian Giraud de Barri (Gerald of Wales) in his Topographia Hibernica."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ox-man-of-wicklow-c02",
      "source_id": "ox-man-of-wicklow-s1",
      "quote": "Selon Giraud, la créature, fruit d'une union contre-nature entre un homme et une vache, avait une grosse tête chauve comme celle d'un bœuf, tandis que son visage était plat et dominé par deux énormes yeux.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Giraud, makhluk itu hasil hubungan tak wajar antara manusia dan sapi, berkepala besar botak seperti lembu, berwajah datar dengan dua mata besar.",
        "en": "According to Giraud, the creature, born of an unnatural union of a man and a cow, had a big bald ox-like head and a flat face dominated by two huge eyes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ox-man-of-wicklow-c03",
      "source_id": "ox-man-of-wicklow-s1",
      "quote": "À la place du nez, la créature avait deux orifices qui servaient de narines et émettaient des sons semblables au mugissement d'un taureau. L'homme-bœuf avait des sabots fendus à la place des mains et des pieds.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai ganti hidung ia punya dua lubang yang mengeluarkan bunyi seperti lenguhan banteng, dan tangan serta kakinya berupa kuku belah.",
        "en": "Instead of a nose it had two holes that made sounds like a bull's bellow, and cloven hooves for hands and feet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ox-man-of-wicklow-c04",
      "source_id": "ox-man-of-wicklow-s1",
      "quote": "la créature, qui se rendait régulièrement à la cour du baron cambro-normand Maurice FitzGerald qui se tenait au Château Noir (Black Castle) de Wicklow, sera enlevée et tuée par les Irlandais.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk yang sering datang ke istana baron Maurice FitzGerald di Black Castle, Wicklow, itu akhirnya diculik dan dibunuh orang Irlandia.",
        "en": "The creature, which regularly visited the court of the baron Maurice FitzGerald at Black Castle, Wicklow, was eventually seized and killed by the Irish."
      }
    }
  ],

        "en": "The Ozena (Latin Ozaena) is a legendary octopus described by Pliny the Elder; its name means \"stinking octopus\" for its foul smell."

      "id": "ozena-c02",
      "source_id": "ozena-s1",
      "quote": "La maggior parte delle ozene era di piccole dimensioni e rimaneva sul fondo del mare. In casi rari alcune specie più grandi aggredivano gli uomini facendoli annegare.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",

    "en": "The Pardalo is a magical wild ass of the Portuguese legend of the Dama Pé-de-Cabra, a wind-swift mount stopped only by a stone cross.",

    {
      "id": "Pardalo adalah keledai liar ajaib dalam legenda Portugis Dama Pé-de-Cabra. Sang dama mengirimnya kepada putranya, D. Inigo, untuk menyelamatkan sang ayah, D. Diogo, yang ditawan orang Moor. Pardalo melintasi gunung dan lembah secepat angin, melompati dua puluh sungai sekali lompat, terbang seperti awan hitam, dan tidak perlu makan.",
      "en": "The Pardalo is a magical wild ass of the Portuguese legend of the Dama Pé-de-Cabra. She sends it to her son, D. Inigo, to rescue his father, D. Diogo, captive of the Moors.       "id": "pardalo-c01",
      "source_id": "pardalo-s1",
      "quote": "O Pardalo é um ser mítico das lendas portuguesas. O Pardalo é um onagro silveste, um ser mágico da lenda da Dama Pé-de-Cabra.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pardalo adalah makhluk mitos legenda Portugis, keledai liar ajaib dalam legenda Dama Pé-de-Cabra.",

    {
      "id": "pardalo-c02",
      "source_id": "pardalo-s1",
      "quote": "Pardalo é a montada que a Dama pé de Cabra envia ao filho D. Inigo para este salvar o pai, D. Diogo, cativo dos mouros.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pardalo adalah tunggangan yang dikirim Dama Pé-de-Cabra kepada putranya, D. Inigo, untuk menyelamatkan ayahnya, D. Diogo, yang ditawan orang Moor.",
        "en": "The Pardalo is the mount the Dama Pé-de-Cabra sends her son D. Inigo to rescue his father, D. Diogo, captive of the Moors."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pardalo-c03",
      "source_id": "pardalo-s1",
      "quote": "O Pardalo percorria distancias incríveis por serras e vales com a rapidez do vento, era capaz de um pulo saltar vinte ribeiras e voava como uma nuvem negra",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pardalo menempuh jarak luar biasa melintasi gunung dan lembah secepat angin, dapat melompati dua puluh sungai sekali lompat, dan terbang seperti awan hitam.",
        "en": "The Pardalo covered incredible distances over mountains and valleys as fast as the wind, could leap twenty streams in one bound and flew like a black cloud."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pardalo-c04",
      "source_id": "pardalo-s1",
      "quote": "No entanto, um simples cruzeiro de pedra era capaz de fazer o Pardalo parar, rugir como uma besta-fera e tremer como varas verdes.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namun salib batu sederhana dapat membuat Pardalo berhenti, meraung seperti binatang buas, dan gemetar.",

      "source_id": "pardalo-s1",
      "quote": "O Pardalo é uma montada guerreira, veloz, forte, que não necessita de alimento",

    {
      "id": "Pardalot adalah momok penakut anak dalam imajinasi rakyat Valencia, makhluk yang tinggal di cerobong bata pabrik di Ribera Baixa dan Plana Baixa. Menurut Francesc Gisbert, ia menikmati panas api dan bersembunyi di balik asap cerobong. Konon ia memakan anak-anak yang terlalu lama bermain sendirian di tempat sepi, sehingga orang tua memakainya untuk menakut-nakuti anak.",
      "en": "The Pardalot is a child-scaring bogey of Valencian folk imagination, a creature living in the brick chimneys of factories in the Ribera Baixa and Plana Baixa.       "quote": "El Pardalot és un espantamainades propi de l'imaginari popular valencià. Es tracta d'una criatura aterridora que vivia a l'interior de les xemeneies de rajola de les fàbriques que s'ubicaven a la zona de la Ribera Baixa i també a la Plana Baixa.",

        "en": "The Pardalot is a child-scaring bogey of Valencian folk imagination, a terrifying creature living inside the brick chimneys of factories in the Ribera Baixa and Plana Baixa."

    },
    {
      "id": "pardalot-c02",
      "source_id": "pardalot-s1",

      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Francesc Gisbert, monster itu menikmati panas api dan memakai asap cerobong untuk menyamarkan diri saat keluar-masuk sarangnya.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",

        "en": "Legend says the Pardalot fed itself and its chicks on children it snatched for wandering alone too long in empty places."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Pare Esmè adalah raksasa Montseny dalam mitologi Katalan yang dapat berdiri di dua puncak sekaligus dan meminum seluruh air sungai.",
    "en": "Pare Esmè is the giant of the Montseny in Catalan mythology, able to stand on two peaks at once and drink a whole river.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "pare-esme-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, Pare Esmè adalah raksasa Montseny yang menari dan melompat di lembah dan pegunungan. Ia begitu tinggi sehingga dapat menapakkan satu kaki di Les Agudes dan satu lagi di Turó de l'Home, dan saat haus ia meminum seluruh air sungai. Beberapa legendanya mirip legenda Fort Farell dan Sagal Nou.",
      "en": "In Catalan mythology Pare Esmè is the giant of the Montseny, dancing and leaping across valleys and ranges. He is so tall he can stand with one foot on Les Agudes and the other on the Turó de l'Home, and when thirsty he drinks a whole river. Some of his legends resemble those of Fort Farell and the Sagal Nou.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pare-esme-c01",
        "pare-esme-c02"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "pare-esme-c01",
      "source_id": "pare-esme-s1",
      "quote": "El Pare Esmè és, en la mitologia catalana, el gegant del Montseny. Se'l descriu com un gegant que balla i salta per les valls i serralades, que pot tenir un peu a les Agudes i l'altre al Turó de l'Home. Era molt alt i quan tenia set es bevia tota l'aigua del riu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, Pare Esmè adalah raksasa Montseny yang menari dan melompat di lembah dan pegunungan, dapat menapakkan satu kaki di Les Agudes dan satu lagi di Turó de l'Home, dan meminum seluruh air sungai saat haus.",
        "en": "In Catalan mythology Pare Esmè is the giant of the Montseny, who dances and leaps across valleys and ranges, can stand with one foot on Les Agudes and the other on the Turó de l'Home, and drinks a whole river when thirsty."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pare-esme-c02",
      "source_id": "pare-esme-s1",
      "quote": "Algunes llegendes relacionades amb aquest gegant tenen similituds amb les del Fort Farell de Caldes de Montbui o el Sagal Nou de l'Urgell.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Beberapa legendanya mirip legenda Fort Farell dari Caldes de Montbui atau Sagal Nou dari Urgell.",
        "en": "Some of its legends resemble those of Fort Farell of Caldes de Montbui or the Sagal Nou of Urgell."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Peeira adalah gadis penjaga serigala dalam legenda Portugal dan Galisia yang dapat berbicara dengan dan mengendalikan kawanan serigala.",
    "en": "The peeira is a wolf-keeping maiden of the legends of Portugal and Galicia who can talk to and command wolf packs.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "peeira-mythological-creature-c01",
      "peeira-mythological-creature-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Peeira, Pijeira, atau Peri Serigala adalah sebutan dalam legenda Portugal dan Galisia bagi gadis yang menjadi penjaga atau teman serigala, versi manusia dan perempuan dari manusia serigala. Ia wajib menyiapkan makanan bagi serigalanya dan dapat berkomunikasi dengan serta mengendalikan kawanan serigala.",
      "en": "Peeira, Pijeira or Wolf Fairy is the name in the legends of Portugal and Galicia for a young woman who becomes the keeper or companion of wolves, the human, female counterpart of the werewolf. She must feed her wolves and can communicate with and control wolf packs.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "peeira-mythological-creature-c01",
        "peeira-mythological-creature-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Seorang gadis konon menjadi peeira bila dipanggil lolongan serigala takdirnya. Dalam versi lain, putri ketujuh ditakdirkan menjaga serigala, hutan, dan gunung selama tujuh tahun, kecuali bila salah satu kakaknya menjadi ibu baptisnya.",
      "en": "A girl is said to become a peeira when called by her destined wolf's howl. In another version a seventh daughter is fated to guard wolves, forests and hills for seven years unless one of her sisters is her godmother.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "peeira-mythological-creature-c03",
        "peeira-mythological-creature-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],

    "id": "Peladits adalah momok Katalan dari Òdena yang menghukum anak-anak yang tidak mau mandi dengan air mendidih dan batu apung.",
    "en": "The Peladits is a Catalan bogey from Òdena that punishes children who won't wash with boiling water and pumice.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "peladits-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Peladits adalah momok penakut anak dari Òdena (Anoia) dalam imajinasi rakyat Katalan. Makhluk jahat ini bersembunyi dekat Montserrat dan menghukum anak yang tidak mau mandi dengan merendamnya di air mendidih dan menggosoknya dengan batu apung. Menurut Joan Amades (1957), ia tinggi kurus, hitam seperti arang, dan berbulu seperti beruang.",
      "en": "The Peladits is a child-scaring bogey from Òdena (Anoia) in Catalan folk imagination. This evil being hides near Montserrat and punishes children who won't wash by bathing them in boiling water and scrubbing them with pumice. According to Joan Amades (1957) it is tall and thin, black as coal and hairy as a bear.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "peladits-c01",
        "peladits-c02"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "source_id": "peladits-s1",
      "quote": "El Peladits és un espantamainades propi de l'imaginari popular català, concretament d'Òdena (Anoia). Es tracta d'un ésser malèfic que viu amagat prop de la muntanya de Montserrat i castiga els nens que no es volen rentar amb terribles suplicis, com ara banyar-los en aigua bullent i fregar-los amb pedra tosca.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Peladits adalah momok penakut anak dari Òdena (Anoia) dalam imajinasi rakyat Katalan, makhluk jahat yang bersembunyi dekat Montserrat dan menghukum anak yang tidak mau mandi dengan merendamnya di air mendidih dan menggosoknya dengan batu apung.",
        "en": "The Peladits is a child-scaring bogey from Òdena (Anoia) in Catalan folk imagination, an evil being hiding near Montserrat that punishes children who won't wash by bathing them in boiling water and scrubbing them with pumice."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "peladits-c02",
      "source_id": "peladits-s1",
      "quote": "Segons va recollir Joan Amades en un article a la Revista de Dialectología y Tradiciones Populares el 1957, el Peladits és alt i prim, negre com el carbó i pelut com un os.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut catatan Joan Amades dalam Revista de Dialectología y Tradiciones Populares (1957), Peladits tinggi kurus, hitam seperti arang, dan berbulu seperti beruang.",
        "en": "As Joan Amades recorded in the Revista de Dialectología y Tradiciones Populares (1957), the Peladits is tall and thin, black as coal and hairy as a bear."

    "id": "Pericó adalah roh besi dalam cerita rakyat Katalan yang kecil dan hampir tak terlihat, tetapi tumbuh menjadi raksasa besi bila terkena api.",
    "en": "The pericó is the iron spirit of Catalan folklore, tiny and nearly invisible, that grows into an iron giant when touched by fire.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "perico-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam imajinasi rakyat Katalan, pericó adalah roh logam, jiwa besi, yang terutama dikenal di Ripollès dan Cerdanya. Ia sangat kecil dan hampir tak terlihat, tetapi bila terkena api ia marah, melompat ke dalam nyala, dan tumbuh menjadi raksasa besi pejal yang menghancurkan segalanya. Karena itu para pandai besi dulu memukul landasan tiga kali sebelum bekerja agar ia tidak keluar dari besi.",
      "en": "In Catalan folk imagination the pericó is the spirit of metal, the soul of iron, known especially in Ripollès and Cerdanya. It is tiny and almost invisible, but fire angers it: it leaps into the flames and swells into a solid-iron giant that crushes everything. So blacksmiths used to strike the anvil three times before work to keep it inside the iron.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "perico-c01",
        "perico-c02"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "source_id": "perico-s1",
      "quote": "El pericó és, dins l'imaginari popular català, el geni del metall, l'ànima del ferro. Especialment conegut al Ripollès i la Cerdanya, és molt menut i gairebé invisible, però amb el foc s'enfada, salta dins les flames i creix fins que es converteix en un gegant enorme de ferro massís, que tot ho esmicola i tot ho aixafa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam imajinasi rakyat Katalan, pericó adalah roh logam, jiwa besi, terutama dikenal di Ripollès dan Cerdanya; ia sangat kecil dan hampir tak terlihat, tetapi bila dibakar ia marah, melompat ke api, dan tumbuh menjadi raksasa besi pejal yang menghancurkan segalanya.",

      }
    },
    {
      "id": "perico-c02",
      "source_id": "perico-s1",
      "quote": "Antigament, els ferrers, abans de començar la feina, donaven tres cops de mall sobre l'enclusa pensant en el Pericó, per tal que no pogués sortir de dins el ferro.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dulu para pandai besi memukul landasan tiga kali sambil memikirkan Pericó sebelum bekerja, agar roh itu tidak keluar dari besi.",
        "en": "Blacksmiths used to strike the anvil three times with the hammer, thinking of the Pericó, before starting work, so it could not come out of the iron."
      }
    }

    "id": "Pertenidor adalah arwah penasaran dalam mitologi Katalan, personifikasi gema yang mengulang dalam ratapan apa yang didengarnya.",
    "en": "The pertenidor is a restless soul of Catalan mythology, the personification of the echo, repeating in laments whatever it hears.",
    "claim_ids": [

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Pertenidor adalah sejenis arwah penasaran dalam mitologi Katalan yang terutama dikenal di Vall de Lord dan Solsonès. Mereka mengembara dan mengulang dalam ratapan apa yang mereka dengar. Menurut Joan Amades mereka personifikasi gema, sehingga orang tidak boleh berteriak di tempat yang bergema agar tidak menyiksa mereka.",
      "en": "The pertenidor is a kind of restless soul of Catalan mythology, known especially in the Vall de Lord and the Solsonès. They wander the land repeating in laments whatever they hear. According to Joan Amades they personify the echo, so one should not shout where voices echo, so as not to make them suffer.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pertenidor-c01",
        "pertenidor-c02"
      ]
    }

      "quote": "Un pertenidor és una mena d'ànima en pena propi de la mitologia catalana. Localitzats a la Vall de Lord i al Solsonès en general, els pertenidors volten errants per la terra i repeteixen en planys allò que van sentint.",

        "id": "Pertenidor adalah sejenis arwah penasaran dalam mitologi Katalan, terutama di Vall de Lord dan Solsonès, yang mengembara dan mengulang dalam ratapan apa yang mereka dengar.",
        "en": "A pertenidor is a kind of restless soul of Catalan mythology, found in the Vall de Lord and the Solsonès, that wanders the land repeating in laments whatever it hears."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pertenidor-c02",
      "source_id": "pertenidor-s1",
      "quote": "Segons Joan Amades, els pertenidors són la personificació del ressò, per la qual cosa cal no cridar ni parlar fort allà on la veu ressoni per tal de no fer-los patir.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Joan Amades, pertenidor adalah personifikasi gema, sehingga orang tidak boleh berteriak di tempat yang bergema agar tidak menyiksa mereka.",
        "en": "According to Joan Amades, pertenidors personify the echo, so one must not shout where the voice echoes, so as not to make them suffer."

    "id": "Pettenedda adalah nenek penghuni sumur dalam tradisi Sardinia yang selalu menyisir rambutnya dan menyeret anak-anak ke dasar sumur.",
    "en": "The pettenedda is a well-dwelling old woman of Sardinian tradition who forever combs her hair and drags children into the well.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "pettenedda-c01",
      "pettenedda-c02",
      "pettenedda-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Pettenedda adalah makhluk mitos tradisi Sardinia yang konon tinggal di sumur. Ia dibayangkan sebagai perempuan sangat tua yang terus menyisir rambut kusutnya dengan kuku yang sangat panjang, sehingga namanya berasal dari kata menyisir. Anak yang mendekat konon akan disambar cakarnya dan diseret ke dasar sumur; legenda ini mungkin dibuat para ibu agar anak menjauhi sumur.",
      "en": "The pettenedda is a mythical creature of Sardinian tradition said to live in wells. She is imagined as a very old woman forever combing her tangled hair with very long nails, hence her name, from combing. Children who come near are said to be seized by her claws and dragged to the bottom; mothers probably invented the legend to keep children away from wells.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pettenedda-c01",
        "pettenedda-c02",
        "pettenedda-c03"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

      "en": "An old woman of the wells.",

    {
      "id": "pettenedda-c01",
      "source_id": "pettenedda-s1",
      "quote": "La pettenedda è una creatura mitica che appartiene alla tradizione sarda e che vivrebbe nei pozzi. Probabilmente la leggenda è stata inventata dalle madri per spaventare i bambini e tenerli lontani dai pozzi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pettenedda adalah makhluk mitos tradisi Sardinia yang konon tinggal di sumur; legendanya mungkin dibuat para ibu untuk menakuti anak agar menjauhi sumur.",
        "en": "The pettenedda is a mythical creature of Sardinian tradition said to live in wells; the legend was probably invented by mothers to keep children away from wells."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pettenedda-c02",
      "source_id": "pettenedda-s1",
      "quote": "La pettenedda è sempre stata immaginata come una donna molto vecchia che passa la vita a pettinarsi (da qui il suo nome) con le sue unghie lunghissime",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pettenedda dibayangkan sebagai perempuan sangat tua yang menghabiskan hidupnya menyisir rambut dengan kukunya yang sangat panjang, sehingga dinamai demikian.",
        "en": "The pettenedda is imagined as a very old woman who spends her life combing her hair with her very long nails, hence her name."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pettenedda-c03",
      "source_id": "pettenedda-s1",
      "quote": "la pettenedda li avrebbe agguantati con i suoi enormi artigli e li avrebbe trascinati in fondo al pozzo",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anak yang mendekati sumur konon akan disambar cakarnya yang besar dan diseret ke dasar sumur.",
        "en": "Children who came near would be seized by her huge claws and dragged to the bottom of the well."
      }
    }

      "id": "Porviso atau proviso adalah makhluk mitologi Galisia, semacam trasno atau iblis, yang muncul pada malam hari dalam wujud berbagai hewan dan menghalangi napas orang yang tidur. Ia berekor panjang dan sangat berbulu. Orang mengusirnya dengan tanda salib atau dengan \"bunga air\" yang dikumpulkan pada pagi hari Santo Yohanes.",
      "en": "The porviso or proviso is a being of Galician mythology, a trasno or demon, that appears at night in various animal forms and stops sleepers from breathing. It has a long tail and is very hairy. People drive it off by crossing themselves or with the \"water flower\" gathered on the morning of St John's day.",

    {
      "id": "porviso-c01",
      "source_id": "porviso-s1",
      "quote": "O porviso ou proviso é un ser da mitoloxía galega, un trasno ou un demo, que aparece de noite adoptando forma de animal, e impídelle alentar á xente mentres dorme.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Porviso atau proviso adalah makhluk mitologi Galisia, semacam trasno atau iblis, yang muncul pada malam hari dalam wujud hewan dan menghalangi napas orang yang tidur.",
        "en": "The porviso or proviso is a being of Galician mythology, a trasno or demon, that appears at night in animal form and stops people breathing in their sleep."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "porviso-c02",
      "source_id": "porviso-s1",
      "quote": "Represéntase cun rabo longo, adopta a forma de distintos animais e é moi peludo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan berekor panjang, sangat berbulu, dan berwujud berbagai hewan.",
        "en": "It is shown with a long tail, very hairy, taking the form of various animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "porviso-c03",
      "source_id": "porviso-s1",
      "quote": "Para escorrentalo hai que persignarse; a \"flor da auga\", que se forma a noite de san Xoán e se recolle pola mañanciña, serve para arredar o porviso, ó igual que todos os demos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk mengusirnya orang membuat tanda salib; \"bunga air\" yang terbentuk pada malam Santo Yohanes dan dikumpulkan pagi-pagi juga mengusir porviso seperti semua iblis.",
        "en": "To drive it away one crosses oneself; the \"water flower\" formed on St John's night and gathered at dawn also wards off the porviso, like all demons."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "porviso-c04",
      "source_id": "porviso-s1",
      "quote": "Rafael Dieste en A fiestra valdeira (1927) nomea o porviso como diaño",

Bisanya membuat tubuh korban membengkak hingga mati dan membusuk, dan Albertus Magnus menggolongkannya di antara ular yang gigitannya paling mematikan, fatal dalam tiga jam tanpa obat.",
      "en": "The prestere is a legendary serpent of medieval bestiaries. Its mouth is always open and gives off vapours, even as it moves swiftly.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "prestere-c01",
      "source_id": "prestere-s1",
      "quote": "Il prestere o prestero (dal latino prester) è un serpente leggendario descritto nei bestiari medievali.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Prestere (dari bahasa Latin prester) adalah ular legendaris dalam bestiarium abad pertengahan.",
        "en": "The prestere (from Latin prester) is a legendary serpent described in medieval bestiaries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "prestere-c02",
      "source_id": "prestere-s1",
      "quote": "In particolare, il veleno del prestere provoca nel corpo della vittima un gonfiore abnorme (con un effetto simile all'idropisia), tale che essa viene uccisa dalla semplice dilatazione dei tessuti, i quali iniziano subito a putrefarsi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bisanya membuat tubuh korban membengkak luar biasa hingga mati karena jaringannya meregang, yang lalu segera membusuk.",
        "en": "Its venom makes the victim's body swell abnormally until it dies from the stretching of its tissues, which at once begin to rot."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "prestere-c03",
      "source_id": "prestere-s1",
      "quote": "Alberto Magno colloca il prestere nel primo ordine degli aspidi, ossia di quelli dal morso più velenoso, che risulta fatale entro tre ore e per il quale non si conoscono cure.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Albertus Magnus menggolongkannya dalam tingkat pertama aspis, yang gigitannya paling berbisa, mematikan dalam tiga jam, dan tak ada obatnya.",
        "en": "Albertus Magnus placed it in the first order of asps, whose bite is most venomous, fatal within three hours and without cure."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "prestere-c04",
      "source_id": "prestere-s1",
      "quote": "La caratteristica più peculiare del prestere è la bocca, che emette vapori e che esso tiene sempre aperta, anche quando si muove, cosa che fa con rapidità.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ciri paling khasnya adalah mulut yang mengeluarkan uap dan selalu terbuka, bahkan saat ia bergerak cepat.",
        "en": "Its most peculiar feature is its mouth, which gives off vapours and stays open even as it moves swiftly."
      }
    }
  ],

      "id": "Pretinho do barrete encarnado, \"si hitam kecil bertopi merah\", adalah makhluk mitologi anak-anak Portugis yang baik hati tetapi nakal.       "en": "The pretinho do barrete encarnado, \"little black one in the red cap\", is a kindly but mischievous being of Portuguese children's mythology.       "quote": "O pretinho do barrete encarnado é um ser da mitologia infantil portuguesa. O pretinho do barrete encarnado é um ser benfazejo travesso e provocador.",
      "locator": null,

      "statement": {
        "id": "Pretinho do barrete encarnado adalah makhluk mitologi anak-anak Portugis yang baik hati, nakal, dan suka menggoda.",

      "id": "pretinho-do-barrete-encarnado-c02",
      "source_id": "pretinho-do-barrete-encarnado-s1",
      "quote": "O pretinho do barrete encarnado aparece sempre à hora de maior calma. É uma entidade graciosa que faz figas e pirraças às crianças para as enraivecer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia selalu muncul pada jam paling tenang dan suka mengejek serta mengusili anak-anak agar mereka kesal.",
        "en": "It always appears at the calmest hour and pulls faces and plays tricks on children to annoy them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pretinho-do-barrete-encarnado-c03",
      "source_id": "pretinho-do-barrete-encarnado-s1",
      "quote": "Diz-se que é o filho mais novo do Diabo, que desobedeceu ao pai por ser amigo das almas boas, principalmente das crianças.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon ia putra bungsu Iblis yang membangkang kepada ayahnya karena bersahabat dengan jiwa-jiwa baik, terutama anak-anak.",
        "en": "It is said to be the Devil's youngest son, who disobeyed his father out of friendship for good souls, especially children."
      }

      "id": "Rosemunho atau remoinho adalah makhluk mitos cerita rakyat Portugis yang terkait mitos matahari: ia muncul pada tengah hari atau pada jam-jam terbuka yang disebut Trindades, dan dianggap jahat. Seperti balborinho, ia tampil sebagai pusaran angin yang merusak kehidupan orang, tetapi berbeda dengannya, asal-usul rosemunho tidak diketahui.",
      "en": "The rosemunho or remoinho is a mythical being of Portuguese folklore tied to the solar myth: it appears at noon or at the open hours called Trindades and is considered evil.     "id": "Teiniaguá atau Salamanca do Jarau adalah putri Moor dalam legenda gaúcho yang diubah menjadi kadal berkepala permata merah delima dan tinggal di Cerro do Jarau.",
    "en": "Teiniaguá, or Salamanca do Jarau, is a Moorish princess of gaúcho legend turned into a lizard with a ruby-gem head, living on Cerro do Jarau.",

    {
      "id": "Legenda Teiniaguá atau Salamanca do Jarau dari budaya gaúcho di Rio Grande do Sul berkisah tentang putri Moor yang berubah menjadi penyihir dan datang dalam sebuah guci dari Salamanca, Spanyol, ke gua di Cerro do Jarau. Iblis Merah orang pribumi, Anhangá-Pitã, mengubahnya menjadi kadal bertubuh salamander dengan batu permata merah delima sebagai kepala, yang memikat lelaki, dan ia ditakdirkan hidup di danau di bukit itu.",
      "en": "The legend of Teiniaguá or Salamanca do Jarau from the gaúcho culture of Rio Grande do Sul tells of a Moorish princess who becomes a witch and comes in an urn from Salamanca, Spain, to a cave on Cerro do Jarau.
