      "quote": "Salina est une figure folklorique franc-comtoise. Elle aurait, selon la légende, donnée son nom à la ville de Salins-les-Bains, dans le département français du Jura.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salina adalah tokoh cerita rakyat Franche-Comté yang menurut legenda memberi nama pada kota Salins-les-Bains di departemen Jura, Prancis.",
        "en": "Salina is a folk figure of Franche-Comté who, in legend, gave her name to the town of Salins-les-Bains in the French department of Jura."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "salina-c02",
      "source_id": "salina-s1",
      "quote": "Salina serait née de l'amour d'une nymphe et d'Apollon, aussi connu sous le nom romain de Phébus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salina konon lahir dari cinta seorang nimfa dan Apollo, yang juga dikenal dengan nama Romawi Phoebus.",
        "en": "Salina was said to be born of the love of a nymph and Apollo, also known by the Roman name Phoebus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "salina-c03",
      "source_id": "salina-s1",
      "quote": "le dieu du soleil décide de lui transmettre le pouvoir d'extraire le sel de la mer, afin de l'offrir aux hommes",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dewa matahari memberinya kemampuan mengambil garam dari laut untuk dipersembahkan kepada manusia.",
        "en": "The sun god gave her the power to extract salt from the sea to offer to humankind."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "salina-c04",
      "source_id": "salina-s1",
      "quote": "À l'occasion de l'anniversaire de Neptune, Salina lui offre de son sel.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada ulang tahun Neptunus, Salina mempersembahkan garamnya kepadanya.",
        "en": "On Neptune's birthday Salina offers him some of her salt."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Satyrus adalah kera legendaris dari bestiarium abad pertengahan yang selalu melahirkan kembar, dan pernah dimuat Linnaeus dalam Systema Naturae.",
    "en": "The satyrus is a legendary ape of medieval bestiaries that always bears twins, once included by Linnaeus in his Systema Naturae.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "satyrus-ape-c01",
      "satyrus-ape-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Satyrus adalah jenis kera dalam beberapa bestiarium abad pertengahan, lincah dan berwajah menyenangkan, yang selalu melahirkan anak kembar dan membenci salah satunya sementara menyayangi yang lain. Ia dimuat dalam edisi awal Systema Naturae karya Carl Linnaeus di bawah Animalia Paradoxa, dan Linnaeus menyebutnya berbulu, berjanggut, bertubuh mirip manusia, serta sejenis monyet bila memang pernah terlihat.",
      "en": "The satyrus is a kind of ape in some medieval bestiaries, lively and pleasant-faced, that always bears twins and hates one while loving the other. It appeared in early editions of Carl Linnaeus's Systema Naturae under Animalia Paradoxa, where Linnaeus called it hairy, bearded, manlike in body and a kind of monkey if ever one was seen.",

    {
      "id": "satyrus-ape-c01",
      "source_id": "satyrus-ape-s1",
      "quote": "is a species of ape described in some medieval bestiaries. It is said to always give birth to twins. Of these twins, it hates one, but loves the other.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Satyrus adalah jenis kera dalam beberapa bestiarium abad pertengahan yang selalu melahirkan anak kembar, membenci salah satunya dan menyayangi yang lain.",
        "en": "The satyrus is a kind of ape in some medieval bestiaries that always bears twins, hating one and loving the other."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "satyrus-ape-c02",
      "source_id": "satyrus-ape-s1",
      "quote": "The ape is also described as lively and having a pleasant face.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kera ini juga digambarkan lincah dan berwajah menyenangkan.",
        "en": "The ape is also described as lively, with a pleasant face."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "satyrus-ape-c03",
      "source_id": "satyrus-ape-s1",
      "quote": "The satyrus was one of many creatures from folklore included in early editions of Carl Linnaeus' Systema Naturae, alongside other legendary creatures, under the wastebasket taxon of Animalia Paradoxa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Satyrus termasuk makhluk cerita rakyat yang dimuat dalam edisi awal Systema Naturae karya Carl Linnaeus, di bawah kelompok Animalia Paradoxa.",
        "en": "The satyrus was among the folklore creatures in early editions of Carl Linnaeus's Systema Naturae, under the catch-all Animalia Paradoxa."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "satyrus-ape-c04",
      "source_id": "satyrus-ape-s1",
      "quote": "Linnaeus is quoted as saying that the satyrus is \"hairy, bearded, with a manlike body, gesticulating much, very fallacious, is a species of monkey, if ever one has been seen.\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Linnaeus menyebut satyrus berbulu, berjanggut, bertubuh mirip manusia, banyak bergerak, sangat menipu, dan sejenis monyet bila memang pernah terlihat.",
        "en": "Linnaeus described the satyrus as hairy, bearded, manlike in body, much gesticulating, very deceptive, and a kind of monkey if ever one was seen."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Secular das nuvens adalah makhluk badai dalam cerita rakyat Portugis yang membuat kilat, guntur, dan badai dari atas awan.",
    "en": "The Secular das nuvens is a storm being of Portuguese folklore who makes lightning, thunder and storms from the clouds.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "secular-das-nuvens-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Secular das nuvens adalah makhluk mitos cerita rakyat Portugis yang membuat badai, bertarung di awan, menciptakan kilat dan guntur, dan membawa badai ke mana pun ia mau. Di Galisia para escolares dipercaya sebagai orang-orang sangat bijak yang naik ke awan dan membuat guntur.",
      "en": "The Secular das nuvens is a mythical being of Portuguese folklore who makes storms, fights in the clouds, creates lightning and thunder and drives storms wherever he likes. In Galicia the escolares are believed to have been very wise men who rose into the clouds and made thunder.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "secular-das-nuvens-c01",
        "secular-das-nuvens-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut kepercayaan, ia terbentuk bila seseorang dibunuh perlahan-lahan, dan menurut Constantino Cabal sosok ini merujuk pada para pelajar sihir di Gua Salamanca.",
      "en": "By belief it is formed when a man is killed slowly, and according to Constantino Cabal the figure refers to the students of magic at the Cave of Salamanca.",

      "id": "secular-das-nuvens-c01",
      "source_id": "secular-das-nuvens-s1",
      "quote": "O Secular das nuvens, Escolar das nuvens ou Escolarão é um ser mítico do folclore português. O Secular das Nuvens é quem faz as tempestades, anda pelas nuvens a batalhar, faz o raio e o trovão, faz as tempestades e leva-as para onde ele quer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Secular das nuvens (juga Escolar das nuvens atau Escolarão) adalah makhluk mitos cerita rakyat Portugis yang membuat badai, bertarung di awan, menciptakan kilat dan guntur, dan membawa badai ke mana pun ia mau.",
        "en": "The Secular das nuvens (also Escolar das nuvens or Escolarão) is a mythical being of Portuguese folklore who makes storms, fights in the clouds, creates lightning and thunder and drives storms wherever he likes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "secular-das-nuvens-c02",
      "source_id": "secular-das-nuvens-s1",
      "quote": "Na Galiza crê-se que os escolares «eram homens muito sábios que subiam às nuvens e faziam trovejar».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Galisia dipercaya bahwa para escolares adalah orang-orang sangat bijak yang naik ke awan dan membuat guntur.",
        "en": "In Galicia the escolares are believed to have been very wise men who rose to the clouds and made it thunder."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "secular-das-nuvens-c03",
      "source_id": "secular-das-nuvens-s1",
      "quote": "Segundo a crença, para formar um secular das nuvens basta matar um homem, mas devagar e por partes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kepercayaan, secular das nuvens terbentuk bila seseorang dibunuh perlahan-lahan sebagian demi sebagian.",

Ular di hutan Devesa itu berambut panjang dan memakai intan besar berkilau di kepalanya, yang hanya dilepasnya saat minum di sungai.       "en": "The Serpent de Manlleu is a legend of the town of Manlleu in Osona. The serpent of the Devesa woods had long hair and a large shining diamond on its head, which it took off only to drink at the river.       "source_id": "serpent-de-manlleu-s1",
      "quote": "La llegenda descriu com una serp (o \"serpent\") que vivia al bosc de la Devesa, prop del nucli urbà, que tenia una llarga cabellera i un diamant gros i lluent al cap, que només es treia per anar a abeurar-se al riu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda menggambarkan seekor ular di hutan Devesa dekat kota yang berambut panjang dan memiliki intan besar berkilau di kepalanya, yang hanya dilepas saat minum di sungai.",
        "en": "The legend tells of a serpent in the Devesa woods near town with long hair and a large shining diamond on its head, which it removed only to drink at the river."
      }

    {
      "id": "serpent-de-manlleu-c04",
      "source_id": "serpent-de-manlleu-s1",
      "quote": "Un noi que volia aconseguir el diamant va estudiar els costums de l'animal i quan el serpent deixà el diamant, l'agafà i sortí corrents. Veient que el serpent el perseguia va entrar dins una casa i va amagar el diamant en un morter posat a l'inrevés",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seorang pemuda mempelajari kebiasaan ular itu, merebut intannya saat dilepas, lalu lari ke sebuah rumah dan menyembunyikan intan itu di bawah lesung terbalik.",
        "en": "A youth studied the serpent's habits, grabbed the diamond when it was set aside, fled into a house and hid it under an upturned mortar."
      }
    }

  "disposition": null,
  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Serpente regolo adalah ular besar berkepala sebesar kepala anak dalam tradisi Toskana dan Umbria, yang namanya berarti \"raja kecil\".",
    "en": "The serpente regolo is a big serpent with a child-sized head in Tuscan and Umbrian tradition, its name meaning \"little king\".",
    "claim_ids": [
      "serpente-regolo-c01",
      "serpente-regolo-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Regolo adalah hewan ajaib dalam tradisi Toskana, Umbria, Abruzzo, Lazio, dan Marche: ular besar berkepala sebesar kepala anak kecil yang hidup di semak, ladang, dan jurang pegunungan. Namanya berarti \"raja kecil\" karena termasuk tradisi basilisk di Mediterania.",
      "en": "The regolo is a fantastic animal of Tuscan, Umbrian, Abruzzese, Lazian and Marchigian tradition: a big serpent with a head as large as a child's, living in thickets, fields and mountain gorges. Its name means \"little king\", as it belongs to the Mediterranean basilisk tradition.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "serpente-regolo-c01",
        "serpente-regolo-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kesaksian tentang dewa bernama Serpente Regolo konon berasal dari zaman Romawi, dan Dio Cassius serta Plinius Tua menceritakan pertemuan pasukan Atilius Regulus dengan reptil raksasa di Afrika pada Perang Punik Pertama.",
      "en": "Testimonies of a deity called Serpente Regolo supposedly go back to Roman times, and Cassius Dio and Pliny the Elder told of Atilius Regulus's army meeting a huge reptile in Africa during the First Punic War.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "serpente-regolo-c03",
        "serpente-regolo-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

      "id": "serpente-regolo-c01",
      "source_id": "serpente-regolo-s1",
      "quote": "Il regolo è un animale fantastico della tradizione toscana, umbra, abruzzese, laziale e delle Marche. Si tratterebbe di un grosso serpente, dalla testa grande «come quella di un bambino», che vive per le macchie, i campi e gli orridi dei monti.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Regolo adalah hewan ajaib dalam tradisi Toskana, Umbria, Abruzzo, Lazio, dan Marche: ular besar berkepala sebesar kepala anak kecil yang hidup di semak, ladang, dan jurang pegunungan.",
        "en": "The regolo is a fantastic animal of Tuscan, Umbrian, Abruzzese, Lazian and Marchigian tradition: a big serpent with a head as large as a child's, living in thickets, fields and mountain gorges."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "serpente-regolo-c02",
      "source_id": "serpente-regolo-s1",
      "quote": "Il nome regolo avrebbe il significato di «piccolo Re», perché appartenente alla tradizione mediterranea del basilisco",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama regolo berarti \"raja kecil\", karena termasuk tradisi basilisk di Mediterania.",
        "en": "The name regolo means \"little king\", as it belongs to the Mediterranean basilisk tradition."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "serpente-regolo-c03",
      "source_id": "serpente-regolo-s1",
      "quote": "Testimonianze di una divinità chiamata Serpente Regolo risalirebbero all'epoca romana.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kesaksian tentang dewa bernama Serpente Regolo konon berasal dari zaman Romawi.",
        "en": "Testimonies of a deity called Serpente Regolo supposedly date back to Roman times."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "serpente-regolo-c04",
      "source_id": "serpente-regolo-s1",
      "quote": "Autori come Dione Cassio e Plinio il Vecchio descrissero l'incontro con un enorme rettile in cui si imbatté l'esercito di Attilio Regolo in Africa durante la prima guerra punica presso il fiume Bagrada.",

      "statement": {
        "id": "Penulis seperti Dio Cassius dan Plinius Tua menceritakan pertemuan pasukan Atilius Regulus dengan reptil raksasa di Afrika dekat Sungai Bagrada pada Perang Punik Pertama.",
        "en": "Writers such as Cassius Dio and Pliny the Elder described the encounter of Atilius Regulus's army with a huge reptile in Africa by the Bagrada river during the First Punic War."
